                    win.focus();
                });
                clearInterval(waitForTheVar);


            }, 500);


        };

<script type="text/javascript">
  var amazonPayButtons = document.querySelectorAll(".amzn-pay-button")

  if (amazonPayButtons.length > 0) {
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search)
    const amazonLogout = urlParams.get("amazon_logout")

    window.onAmazonLoginReady = function () {
      if (amazonLogout === "true") {
          window.amazon.Login.logout()
          console.log("logged out amazon")
      }

      try {
        amazon.Login.setClientId("amzn1.application-oa2-client.3ad5b9e873e14cdf955b55f90ebacaba");
        amazon.Login.setUseCookie(true);
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(err);
      }
    };

    window.onAmazonPaymentsReady = function () {
      var authRequest;

      Array().forEach.call(amazonPayButtons, function(element) {
        OffAmazonPayments.Button(element.id, "A2YVZ4X2OLJI7V", {
          type: "LwA",
          color: "DarkGray",
          size: "medium",
          language: "it-IT",
          authorization: function() {
            loginOptions = { scope: "profile postal_code payments:widget payments:shipping_address", popup: true };
            authRequest = amazon.Login.authorize(loginOptions, "index.php?main_page=checkout_amzn&amz_type=LwA");
          },
          onError: function(error) {
            console.log(error)
          }
        });
      })
    }
  }
</script>
